self.log_message(f"✅ socks-proxy.net: найдено {len(proxies)}")\n        except Exception as e:\n            self.log_message(f"⚠️ Ошибка socks-proxy.net: {e}")\n        return proxies\n\n    def parse_proxy_list_download(self):\n        proxies = []\n        try:\n            url = "https://www.proxy-list.download/api/v1/get?type=http"\n            response = requests.get(url, timeout=15, headers={\'User-Agent\': \'Mozilla/5.0\'})\n            if response.status_code == 200:\n                data = response.json()\n                if \'data\' in data and data[\'data\']:\n                    for item in data[\'data\']:\n                        if \'ip\' in item and \'port\' in item:\n                            proxies.append(f"{item[\'ip\']}:{item[\'port\']}")\n            self.log_message(f"✅ proxy-list.download: найдено {len(proxies)}")\n        except Exception as e:\n            self.log_message(f"⚠️ Ошибка proxy-list.download: {e}")\n        return proxies\n\n    def parse_spys_me(self):\n        proxies = []\n        try:\n            url = "https://spys.me/proxy.txt"\n            response = requests.get(url, timeout=15, headers={\'User-Agent\': \'Mozilla/5.0\'})\n            if response.status_code == 200:\n                lines = response.text.split(\'\\n\')\n                for line in lines:\n                    line = line.strip()\n                    if line and not line.startswith(\'#\') and \':\' in line:\n                        parts = line.split()\n                        if len(parts) >= 1 and \':\' in parts[0]:\n                            proxies.append(parts[0])\n            self.log_message(f"✅ spys.me: найдено {len(proxies)}")\n        except Exception as e:\n            self.log_message(f"⚠️ Ошибка spys.me: {e}")\n        return proxies\n\n    def parse_hidemy_name(self):\n        proxies = []\n        try:\n            url = "https://hidemy.name/en/proxy-list/"\n            response = requests.get(url, timeout=15, headers={\'User-Agent\': \'Mozilla/5.0\'})\n            soup = BeautifulSoup(response.text, \'html.parser\')\n            table = soup.find(\'table\', {\'class\': \'table\'})\n            if table:\n                rows = table.find_all(\'tr\')[1:]\n                for row in rows:\n                    cols = row.find_all(\'td\')\n                    if len(cols) >= 2:\n                        ip = ОСНОВНОЙ ПРОЦЕСС ==========\n    def parse_all(self):\n        try:\n            limit = int(self.limit_entry.get() or 100)\n            timeout = int(self.timeout_entry.get() or 5)\n        except:\n            limit, timeout = 100, 5\n\n        self.log_message(f"🚀 Начинаем асинхронный парсинг прокси (лимит: {limit})")\n\n        all_proxies = []\n        sources_to_parse = []\n\n        for name, var in self.source_vars.items():\n            if var.get():\n                sources_to_parse.append(name)\n\n        if not sources_to_parse:\n            self.log_message("⚠️ Выберите хотя бы один источник!")\n            self.start_btn.configure(state="normal")\n            self.is_running = False\n            return\n\n        self.log_message(f"📡 Источники: {\', \'.join(sources_to_parse)}")\n\n        parse_functions = {\n            \'free-proxy-list.net\': self.parse_free_proxy_list,\n            \'sslproxies.org\': self.parse_sslproxies,\n            \'us-proxy.org\': self.parse_us_proxy,\n            \'socks-proxy.net\': self.parse_socks_proxy,\n            \'proxy-list.download\': self.parse_proxy_list_download,\n            \'spys.me\': self.parse_spys_me,\n            \'hidemy.name\': self.parse_hidemy_name,\n        }\n\n        for source in sources_to_parse:\n            if not self.is_running:\n                break\n            if source in parse_functions:\n                proxies = parse_functions[source]()\n                all_proxies.extend(proxies)\n\n        all_proxies = list(dict.fromkeys(all_proxies))\n        self.log_message(f"📊 Найдено всего: {len(all_proxies)} прокси")\n\n        if not all_proxies:\n            self.log_message("⚠️ Прокси не найдены. Проверьте источники.")\n            self.start_btn.configure(state="normal")\n            self.is_running = False\n            return\n\n        try:\n            loop = asyncio.new_event_loop()\n            asyncio.set_event_loop(loop)\n            working = loop.run_until_complete(\n                self.check_proxies_async(all_proxies, timeout, limit)\n            )\n        except Exception as e:\n            self.log_message(f"❌ Ошибка при асинхронной проверке: {e}")\n            working = []\n\n        self.result_text.delete("1.0", "end")\n        if working:\n            self.result_text.insert("1.0", "\\n".join(working))\n            self.proxy_count_label.configure(text=f"Найдено: {len(working)}")\n            self.log_message(f"🎯 Найдено {len(working)} рабочих прокси")\n        else:\n            self.log_message("⚠️ Рабочих прокси не найдено")\n\n        self.status_label.configure(text="Готово")\n        self.start_btn.configure(state="normal")\n        self.is_running = False\n\n    # ========== УПРАВЛЕНИЕ ==========\n    def start_parsing(self):\n        if self.is_running:\n            return\n\n        try:\n            import aiohttp\n        except ImportError:\n            messagebox.showerror("Ошибка", "Установите aiohttp: pip install aiohttp")\n            return\n\n        self.is_running = True\n        self.start_btn.configure(state="disabled")\n        self.log_message("=" * 50)\n        self.log_message("🎯 Запуск асинхронного парсинга")\n\n        self.thread = threading.Thread(target=self.parse_all)\n        self.thread.daemon = True\n        self.thread.start()\n\n    def stop_parsing(self):\n        if self.is_running:\n            self.is_running = False\n            self.log_message("⏹ Остановлено")\n            self.start_btn.configure(state="normal")\n            self.status_label.configure(text="Остановлено")\n\n    def run(self):\n        self.window.mainloop()\n\nif __name__ == "__main__":\n    if sys.platform == \'win32\':\n        asyncio.set_event_loop_policy(asyncio.WindowsSelectorEventLoopPolicy())\n    app = ProxyParserApp()\n    app.run()\n```\n\n---\n\n## 📝 Что изменилось в этом коде:\n\n1. **Добавлена проверка скорости** — теперь парсер отсеивает медленные прокси (отклик > 3 секунд)\n2. **Улучшенная логика** — в логе видно, какие прокси медленные\n3. **Все импорты на месте** — ошибка `name \'requests\' is not defined` исправлена\n\n---\n\n## 🚀 Как использовать:\n\n1. **Сохраните** этот код как `proxy_parser.py`\n2. **Запустите**:\n```bash\npython proxy_parser.py\n```\n3. chrome_options.add_argument("--disable-dev-shm-usage")\n        chrome_options.add_argument("--window-size=1920,1080")\n        \n        # Дополнительные опции для скрытия автоматизации\n def imitate_user_behavior(self, driver, seconds):\n        """Имитирует поведение пользователя на странице"""\n        start_time = time.time()\n        elapsed = 0\n        \n        while elapsed < seconds:\n            # Прокрутка\n            scroll_amount = random.randint(100, 500)\n            driver.execute_script(f"window.scrollBy(0, {scroll_amount});")\n            time.sleep(random.uniform(0.5, 1.5))\n            \n            # Движение мыши\n            x = random.randint(100, 1000)\n            y = random.randint(100, 800)\n            driver.execute_script(f"window.dispatchEvent(new MouseEvent(\'mousemove\', {{clientX: {x}, clientY: {y}}}));")\n            time.sleep(random.uniform(0.3, 0.8))\n            \n            elapsed =
